- **email**: Send a plain-text email from this box via direct-to-MX SMTP (no local MTA, no credentials). Resolves the recipient domain's MX and delivers straight to the receiving mail server over STARTTLS, with a valid Message-ID and Date so Google/RFC-5322 filters accept it. Default sender is claude@&lt;hostname&gt;. Use when asked to email someone from this machine, send a test/notification message, or mail a short report.
